3. Since objective dominated by small lags, we can optimize via descent on top lag pairs: For a given critical lag d, pattern 1->0 at pairs. To reduce, want change a 1 at i to 0 and 0 at j to 1 such that j=i+d (fix that pair) and not create new. 4. Moves that reduce H_d total: swap to make more pairs agree. This is like minimizing sum of weights over lags. Use energy E=∑ w_d g_d. If w emphasize critical lags. A swap's delta can be computed exactly fast for all lags involving the two positions.

For a binary swap: positions a with h=1 ->0, position b with h=0 ->1. Delta of g_L for any lag L:
g_L = ∑_{i: i,i+L valid} h_{i+L}(1-h_i). Contributions involving a/b:

- project_feasible: Use iterative waterfilling to exactly sum target, clip, but maintain exact. Need if near boundaries. Can implement robust:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
 target=n/2
 h=np.clip(h,0,1)
 for _ in range(1000):
   diff=target-np.sum(h)
   if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
   if diff>0:
      free = np.where(h<1)[0]
      if len(free)==0: break
      # add to free proportionally to 1-h to avoid overshoot
      room=1-h[free]
      # If diff <= room.sum(), just add small eps to all free? To avoid overshoot, add t*room.
      t = min(1.0, diff / room.sum() if room.sum()>0 else 1.0)
      h[free] = np.clip(h[free]+t*room[free],0,1)
   else:
      free=np.where(h>0)[0]
      avail=h[free]
      t = min(1.0, -diff/avail.sum() if avail.sum()>0 else 1.0)
      h[free] = np.clip(h[free]+t*avail[free],0,1)
 # then fix rounding exactly by adjusting extremes
```
After loop, sum may off by 1e-12. Could adjust one element. But target half-integer for even; if sum close, set: find element with slack to adjust err. If no slack, adjust multiple. Need exact `sum(h) == n/2 exactly`? The rule says equivalently sum==n/2 exactly, but floating. They likely check np.isclose. But to be safe, after n integer even, adjust a few values to make sum within 1e-12. Could do:
```
err = target - h.sum()
i = int(np.argmin(np.abs(h-0.5)))
h[i] += err*(1 - abs(h[i]-0.5)?) clip.
```
If still not, distribute among 3 elements.

```
def threshold_binary(h,n):
 order=np.argsort(h)[::-1]
 hb=np.zeros(n)
 hb[order[:n//2]]=1
 return hb
```

Let's implement delta for binary swap vectorized:
Given current h (0/1), swap a (1 to 0) and b (0 to 1). Compute new_raw = raw + delta. We don't need full correlate.
For each lag L, raw_L = ∑ h_{i+L}(1-h_i).
Change at a and b. Δ_L = sum over terms in raw_L involving a/b after- before.
A term h_p(1-h_q) where p=i+L, q=i, p-q=L. It is affected if p=a or q=a or p=b or q=b.
We can compute contributions using current values before swap and after. Since a changes 1->0, b 0->1.
General formula with indicator changes Δh_a=-1, Δh_b=+1.
Product term h_p(1-h_q). Derivative wrt h_p =1-h_q, wrt h_q=-h_p. So delta = Δh_p*(1-h_q) + Δh_q*(-h_p). For each pair involving a/b.
So for swap, for each L, affected pairs:
- p=a, q=a-L if valid: delta += -1*(1 - h[q])
- q=a, p=a+L if valid: delta += +? Δh_q for q=a is -1, term derivative wrt q = -h_p, so contribution + h[p] (because Δ=-1 * -h_p = +h_p). Wait delta = Δh_p*(1-h_q)+Δh_q*(-h_p). If q=a, Δh_q=-1 => -Δ? contribution = + h_p.
- p=b, q=b-L: Δh_p=+1 => +(1 - h[q])
- q=b, p=b+L: Δh_q=+1 => -h[p]
If a and b both in same term (p=a,q=b or p=b,q=a), sum contributions; formula works if both valid? Need avoid double? It will accumulate both terms, okay.
Thus Δ_L = sum of up to four terms:
`d_p_a = -1*h[q? no (1-h[q])]` etc. Let's write:
`delta = 0.0`
if valid p=a, q=a-L: delta -= (1 - h[q])
if valid q=a, p=a+L: delta += h[p]
if valid p=b, q=b-L: delta += (1 - h[q])
if valid q=b, p=b+L: delta -= h[p]
Use h before swap.

```
def binary_flip_delta(p, h, v):
    n=len(h); M=2*n-1; d=np.zeros(M)
    delta = -1.0 if v==1 else 1.0
    # p as first factor: L=p-q, k = n-1+p-q, q=0..n-1. positions k from p to p+n-1 increasing? q=n-1..0.
    d[p:p+n] += delta * (1.0 - h[np.arange(n-1, -1, -1)])? But using h reversed vector length n.
    # p as second (q): L=r-p, k = n-1+r-p, r=0..n-1 increasing. positions from n-1-p to 2*n-1-p.
    d[n-1-p : 2*n-1-p] -= delta * h
    # self correction: subtract 1? S=1 always for binary flip? Since old binary, S=1. yes `d[n-1] -= 1.0`
    return d
```

Let's implement `swap_delta(a,b,h,raw_workspace?)` returning new_raw in out maybe:
```
def apply_swap_delta(raw, h, a, b, out):
    # assumes h[a]>0.5, h[b]<0.5
    n=len(h); M=2*n-1
    # out = raw + flip_a + flip_b + cross
    # We'll compute raw in out starting as raw.copy? But out can be temp.
    np.copyto(out, raw)
    # flip a old 1
    piv = h[::-1]; comp_rev = 1.0 - piv
    out[a:a+n] -= comp_rev  # delta=-1 -> - (1-h_q)
    out[n-1-a : 2*n-1-a] += h  # -delta*h? for v=1 delta=-1, -delta=+1
    out[n-1] -= 1.0
    # flip b old 0 delta=+1
    out[b:b+n] += comp_rev
    out[n-1-b : 2*n-1-b] -= h
    out[n-1] -= 1.0
    k1 = n-1 + a - b
    k2 = n-1 + b - a
    out[k1] += 1.0
    out[k2] += 1.0
    return out
```
Check with v=1 flip: p role `d[p:p+n] += delta*(1-h_rev)`: delta=-1 -> subtract comp_rev. q role `d[...]-=delta*h`: -(-1)h=+h. self subtract 1. v=0: p role add comp_rev. q role `-= +1*h` -> subtract h. self subtract 1. OK.
Note: h used original, and we add both flips into out. h remains original for both; OK because cross terms account sequential. But p role for b uses h_rev which includes h[a]=1; cross terms adjust terms involving a? For p role b with q=a, original contribution 0 (since 1-h_a=0), intermediate contribution 1; cross +1 adds. For q role b with p=a, original -h_a=-1, intermediate 0; cross +1 adds. Good. What about q role a with p=b: original -? a flip q role uses +h (because -delta*h) with h_b=0 original, intermediate should 0; OK. p role a with q=b original - (1-h_b)=-1, intermediate after b flip should? a flip first: after a=0, b=0, term 0; no further. cross +1 correct. So formula works.

Let's test with n=2 quickly using code mentally: out start [1,0,0]. a=0,b=1. comp_rev [1,0], h [1,0]
a flip: out[0:2]-=comp ->[0,0,0]? [1,0]-[1,0]=[0,0]; out[1:3]+=h -> [0,1,0]; self out[1]-=1 ->[0,0,0]
b flip: out[1:3]+=comp ->[0,1,0]; out[0:2]-=h ->[-1,1,0]; self out[1]-=1 ->[-1,0,0]
cross k1=0,k2=2 +1 -> [0,0,1]. OK.

Local search algorithm detail:
```
def binary_delta_search(h_init, n, deadline, max_iter, seed):
    h = threshold_binary(h_init,n) maybe
    raw = np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
    cur_max = raw.max()
    best_raw=cur_max; best_h=h.copy()
    rng = RandomState(seed)
    pre = h[::-1]; comp_rev = 1-pre
    tmp = np.empty(M)
    ones = np.where(h==1)[0]; zeros = np.where(h==0)[0]
    improvements=0; streak=0
    for it in range(max_iter):
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       # Refresh critical maybe every 100 or based on cur_max
       if it%50==0:
          crit = np.where(raw >= cur_max - 4)[0]
          if len(crit)==0: crit=np.array([np.argmax(raw)])
       if len(crit)==0 or rng.rand()<0.05: # random
          if len(ones)==0 or len(zeros)==0: break
          a=rng.choice(ones); b=rng.choice(zeros)
       else:
          k=crit[rng.randint(len(crit))]
          L=k-(n-1)
          if L==0:
             continue
          if L>0:
             # p i+L, i 0..n-1-L
             idx=np.nonzero((h[L:]>0.5)&(h[:-L]<0.5))[0]
             if len(idx)==0: continue
             # candidate a=idx+L, b=idx
             sample = rng.randint(len(idx), size=min(20,len(idx)))
          else:
             d=-L
             idx=np.nonzero((h[:-d]>0.5)&(h[d:]>? wait p=i+L=y, q=i. For L=-d, p=i-d, q=i. Need h[p]=1, h[i]=0, q range d..n-1, p 0..n-d-1.
             idx=np.nonzero((h[:n-d]>0.5)&(h[d:]<0.5))[0]
             if len(idx)==0: continue
             # a=idx; b=idx+d
             sample=...
          choose random sample idxe; a,b.
       # ensure a is one, b zero
       if h[a]<=0.5 or h[b]>=0.5: continue
       apply_swap_delta(raw,h,a,b,tmp)
       m = int(round(tmp.max()))? tmp should be near ints.
       # If improvement
       if m < cur_max:
           # accept
           raw = tmp.copy(); h[a]=0;h[b]=1; cur_max=m; improvements++; streak=0
           if cur_max < best_raw: best_raw=cur_max; best_h=h.copy()
           # update ones/zeros? Could use full where each accept (n=250 cheap)
           ones=np.where(h==1)[0]; zeros=np.where(h==0)[0]
       else:
           # equal move to escape? if m==cur_max and streak large or random prob
           if m == cur_max and (streak > 200 or rng.rand()<0.02):
               # to avoid immediate reverse, check not just undo? optional
               raw = tmp.copy(); h[a]=0;h[b]=1; cur_max=m
               ones=zeros update; streak+=1
           else:
               streak +=1
           if streak > 5000:
               # reset to best and random perturb
               h = best_h.copy(); raw=...; ...; streak=0
    return best_h, best_raw
```
But equal moves with no improvement can wander. Need limit. Could only accept equal if it reduces a secondary measure: e.g., sum of top 10 raw or number of critical pairs for a chosen lag. For each L:
```
if L >=0:
    # p role? Using term h_p(1-h_{p-L}), p from L to n-1, q=0..n-L-1. For derivative wrt h_p: (1-h_q); wrt h_q: -h_p.
    # Our earlier gradient formula for L>=0:
    grad[L:n] += a*(1 - h[0:n-L])   # p role (a as higher)
    grad[0:n-L] -= a*h[L:n]          # q role
else: # L=-d
    d=-L
    # p = i+L = i-d, so p from 0..n-d-1, q=i from d..n-1. For derivative wrt p (first factor): (1-h_q) for p=0..n-d-1, q=d..n-1
    grad[0:n+d] += a*(1 - h[d:n])  # since n-d length; n+d? if L=-d, n+L = n-d. Use n - d.
    grad[d:n] -= a*h[0:n-d]       # q role: -h_p for q=d..n-1, p=0..n-d
```

Implement `slsqp_run(h, alpha, maxiter, deadline)`:
```
def slsqp_run(h, n, alpha, maxiter, deadline):
    set globals
    x0=project_feasible(h,n)
    res = minimize(soft_objgrad, x0, method='SLSQP', bounds=_opt_bounds, constraints=..., options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-12})
    if res.success or hasattr: h=res.x
    return project_feasible(h,n)
```

Project function robust:
```
def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.clip(np.asarray(h,dtype=float),0,1)
    target = n/2.0
    for _ in range(200):
        s = h.sum()
        diff = target - s
        if abs(diff) < 1e-12:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = room.sum()
            if tot <= 1e-15: break
            # To avoid overshoot, if diff <= tot, add min(1, diff/tot)*room; but if some at 1, repeated.
            step = 1.0
            # Actually adding diff*room/tot may overshoot because big room? If diff <= tot, t=diff/tot <=1, then new h_i += t room_i, and 1-new ≥(1-t)room_i if? Let's check: h_i+t(1-h_i) =1-(1-t)(1-h_i) <=1. good. So step=1 if diff<tot else step>1? But diff can not exceed tot because max sum n, target<=n. if diff<tot, OK. Use step=diff/tot.
            h = np.clip(h + (diff/tot)*room,0,1)
        else:
            meas = h
            tot = meas.sum()
            if tot <=1e-15: break
            # h + (diff/tot)*h = (1 + diff/tot)h. If diff negative, factor<1, stays nonnegative.
            h = np.clip(h + (diff/tot)*meas,0,1)
    # adjust residual
    h = np.clip(h,0,1)
    diff = target - h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-15:
       # adjust the element closest to 0.5 with slack
       idx = np.argsort(np.abs(h-0.5))[:max(1, min(n,10))]
       # distribute if possible
       for i in idx:
          if abs(diff) < 1e-15: break
          room = 1-h[i] if diff>0 else h[i]
          if room > 1e-16:
             add = diff if abs(diff)<=room else (room if diff>0 else -room)
             h[i] += add; diff -= add
    return h
```
This may converge but if h at bounds and diff large? target feasible. Fine.

Threshold binary:
```
def threshold_binary(h,n):
    hb=np.zeros(n)
    order=np.argsort(h)[::-1]
    hb[order[:n//2]]=1.0
    return hb
```
But if h values tie, arbitrary. For n even.

Resample:
```
def resample(h, n_old, n_new):
    if n_old==n_new: return h.copy()
    dx_old=2.0/n_old; dx_new=2.0/n_new
    x_old=dx_old*(np.arange(n_old)+0.5)
    x_new=dx_new*(np.arange(n_new)+0.5)
    return project_feasible(np.interp(x_new,x_old,h), n_new)
```

`slsqp_polish`:
```
def slsqp_polish(h, n, alpha, maxiter, deadline):
    x0 = project_feasible(h, n)
    _STATE['n']=n; _STATE['dx']=2.0/n; _STATE['alpha']=float(alpha); _STATE['target']=n/2.0
    _STATE['bounds']=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    _STATE['lags']=np.arange(-(n-1), n, dtype=int)
    options={'maxiter':int(maxiter),'ftol':1e-12,'disp':0}
    try:
      res=minimize(soft_objgrad, x0, method='SLSQP', bounds=_STATE['bounds'],
                   constraints=[{'type':'eq','fun':cons_fun,'jac':cons_jac}], options=options)
      if res.x is not None:
        x1=project_feasible(res.x,n)
        return x1
    except Exception:
      return x0
    return x0
```
Need `cons_jac(x)` returns ones but SLSQP expects function of x returns array; top-level uses state n. OK.

`soft_objgrad` details:
```
def soft_objgrad(x):
    n=_STATE['n']; dx=_STATE['dx']; alpha=_STATE['alpha']
    h=np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    # If sum constraint maybe SLSQP; no clip? For robust:
    if h.min() < -1e-8 or h.max() > 1+1e-8:
        h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    raw=np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, 'full')
    g=raw*dx
    gm=float(np.max(g))
    z=alpha*(g-gm)
    ev=np.exp(z - np.max(z)) # z max 0, but safe
    s=ev.sum()
    if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        w=np.full(n, 0.0) ? length M
    F = gm + (np.log(s) if s>0 else 0)/alpha
    grad=np.zeros(n)
    if s>0:
       w=ev/s
       lags=_STATE['lags']
       # To avoid loop over M if alpha high, skip w<1e-12
       for idx in np.nonzero(w > 1e-12)[0]:
           L=int(lags[idx]); a=float(w[idx])*dx
           if L >= 0:
               if L == 0:
                   grad[:] += a*(1.0 - 2.0*h)
               else:
                   grad[L:n] += a*(1.0 - h[:n-L])
                   grad[:n-L] -= a*h[L:n]
           else:
               d=-L
               grad[:n-d] += a*(1.0 - h[d:n])
               grad[d:n] -= a*h[:n-d]
    return F, grad
```
Use M? `ev` length 2n-1. 
Potential `np.log(s)` for s=1 when max unique ->0, F=gm. Good.
Gradient scale: w sum1, a=w*dx. For F = gm + log(s)/alpha. If gm itself depends on h, derivative of gm not exactly captured by w? The softmax logsumexp formula F = logsumexp(alpha g)/alpha. Derivative = (1/alpha) * sum exp(alpha g_i) / sum * alpha ∂g_i = w ∂g_i. We compute F = gm + log(s)/alpha with s=sum exp(alpha(g-gm)). Since gm is a constant if using current gm? But in function evaluation we compute gm as max(g) which depends on h. The derivative of F = max(g) + logsumexp(...)/alpha: If we treat gm as current max not h-dependent, the derivative of the logsumexp part only? Actually F(h)=max_i g_i(h) + (1/alpha) log ∑ exp(alpha(g_i(h)-max_j g_j(h))). This expression's gradient is not simply sum w ∂g_i if max is h-dependent? There is known stable formula: `logsumexp(alpha*g)/alpha`. If we implement `gm = max(g)` and z=alpha*(g-gm), then F=gm + (logsumz)/alpha. But gm is max over h, not a fixed index. Gradients of the shifted logsumexp: ∂F = ∂gm + (1/alpha)∂logsumz. The derivative of gm is ∂g_m where m index of max. The derivative of logsumz includes -alpha gm' because gm inside z. They cancel if derivative of gm =? Let's derive: F = (1/alpha) log ∑ exp(alpha g_i) (independent of gm). If using gm, z_i=alpha(g_i-gm), F=gm + (1/alpha)log ∑ e^{α(g_i-gm)}. Let S=∑ e^{α(g_i-gm)}. ∂F = ∂gm + (1/α) S'/S. S'/S = ∑ w_i α(∂g_i - ∂gm) = α(∑w_i∂g_i - ∂gm∑w_i)=α(∑w_i∂g_i - ∂gm). Thus ∂F=∂gm + ∑w_i∂g_i - ∂gm = ∑w_i∂g_i. So gradient = w ∂g. Good. No extra. The value uses gm, but gradient formula valid. So okay.

But if there are multiple maxima and gm nondifferentiable, w uniform; gradient formula equals expected subgradient? fine.

But if we pass `fun` that returns (f,g) and `jac` not set, SLSQP may try to evaluate `jac` separately? We pass `fun=soft_objgrad` and `jac=True`? For SLSQP, `jac` argument can be callable or `True`? I think `jac=True` tells it `fun` returns 2-tuple. Use `minimize(..., jac=True)`. But if `fun` returns (f,g), and `jac=True`, SLSQP recognizes. If `jac` callable, it expects only gradient? Safer: use `fun=soft_f` (scalar) and `jac=soft_g` (gradient) separately. `binary_delta_search` implementation more detail:
```
def binary_delta_search(h0, n, deadline, max_iter, seed, batch_size=8, near=2):
    h = threshold_binary(h0,n)
    raw = np.correlate(h,1.0-h,'full')
    cur_max = int(raw.max())
    best_h = h.copy(); best_raw=cur_max
    tmp = np.empty(2*n-1)
    M=tmp.size; h_rev = h[::-1]; comp_rev = 1.0 - h_rev
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    ones = np.where(h==1)[0]; zeros=np.where(h==0)[0]
    cur_sec = int(np.sum(raw >= cur_max - near))
    no_improve=0; check=0
    def? no closures. Use top-level? We can implement loops directly.
    for it in range(max_iter):
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       if len(ones)==0 or len(zeros)==0: break
       if cur_max > best_raw: h=best_h.copy(); raw=np.correlate(...); cur_max=best_raw; ones...
       # refresh crit every 100 or near change
       if it % 50 == 0 or near? crit = np.where(raw >= cur_max - max(2, min(10,cur_max//5)))[0]
       best_m=cur_max; best_sec=cur_sec; best_ab=None
       attempts=0
       while attempts < batch_size:
          attempts +=1
          if len(crit)==0:
             a=rng.choice(ones); b=rng.choice(zeros)
          else:
             if rng.rand() < 0.1:
                a=rng.choice(ones); b=rng.choice(zeros)
             else:
                k=crit[rng.randint(len(crit))]
                L=k-(n-1)
                if L==0: continue
                if L > 0:
                    idxs = np.nonzero((h[L:n] > 0.5) & (h[0:n-L] < 0.5))[0]
                    if len(idxs)==0: continue
                    c=idxs[rng.randint(len(idxs))]
                    a=c+L; b=c
                else:
                    d=-L
                    idxs = np.nonzero((h[0:n-d] > 0.5) & (h[d:n] < 0.5))[0]
                    if len(idxs)==0: continue
                    c=idxs[rng.randint(len(idxs))]
                    a=c; b=c+d
          if h[a] <=0.5 or h[b] >=0.5: continue
          apply swap delta to tmp
          m = int(np.round(tmp.max()))
          if m < cur_max or (m == cur_max):
              if m < cur_max:
                 sec = int(np.sum(tmp >= cur_max - near))? For improvement, sec threshold maybe new max? Use near new: `np.sum(tmp >= m - near)`.
              if m < best_m or (m == best_m and sec < best_sec):
                  best_m=m; best_sec=sec; best_ab=(a,b)
       if best_ab is not None:
          a,b=best_ab
          apply delta to tmp; raw=np.rint(tmp).copy() # maybe assign tmp? We can copy tmp to raw.
          h[a]=0.0; h[b]=1.0
          # update h_rev, comp_rev
          h_rev = h[::-1]; comp_rev = 1.0 - h_rev
          cur_max=best_m
          cur_sec = int(np.sum(raw >= cur_max - near))
          ones = np.where(h==1)[0]; zeros = np.where(h==0)[0]
          no_improve=0
          if cur_max < best_raw:
             best_raw=cur_max; best_h=h.copy()
          check +=1
          if check > 64:
             check=0
             raw2=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,'full')
             if abs(int(round(raw2.max())) - cur_max) > 0:
                # reset raw to true
                raw = np.rint(raw2); cur_max=int(raw.max()); cur_sec=...
                # continue (h unchanged)
       else:
          no_improve +=1
          if no_improve > 5000:
             # reset and random perturb
             h = best_h.copy()
             raw = np.correlate(...); cur_max=best_raw; ...
             no_improve=0
             # do 3 random swaps that don't worsen? optional
             for _ in range(5): random swap accept if max <= cur_max
    return best_h, float(best_raw * (2.0/n))
```
Need update `h_rev` after h changes. In `apply_swap_delta`, it uses `h_rev` and `comp_rev` passed? We can make function `apply_swap_into(raw,h,a,b,h_rev,comp_rev,out)`. But h_rev from current h. If we update h after, OK.
Top-level:
```
def apply_swap_delta(raw, h, a, b, h_rev, comp_rev, out):
    n=len(h); M=out.size
    np.copyto(out, raw)
    out[a:a+n] -= comp_rev
    out[n-1-a : 2*n-1-a] += h
    out[n-1] -= 1.0
    out[b:b+n] += comp_rev
    out[n-1-b : 2*n-1-b] -= h
    out[n-1] -= 1.0
    k1=n-1+a-b; k2=n-1+b-a
    out[k1] += 1.0; out[k2] += 1.0
    return out
```
But note if a/b and self corrections for p=n-1? Slices okay. If a or b? `2*n-1-a` upper exclusive; for a=0 upper 2n-1 = M, good. For b=0 same. `n-1-a` lower. 
Check if `out[n-1] -=1` for both flips. What if a and b such that self corrections overlap? a≠b so both flip different self lags? The self lag k=n-1 for both, subtract twice. For a swap, two single flips each have self term 0; subtract 1 each; correct. If h originally maybe a/b? yes.
But if n=1? not.

Detailed `generate_starts` to include:
```
def generate_starts(n, seed, base):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    xs = (2.0/n)*(np.arange(n)+0.5)
    starts=[]
    if base is not None:
       base=project_feasible(base,n)
       starts.append(base)
       starts.append(project_feasible(0.5*(base+base[::-1]),n))
       starts.append(threshold_binary(base,n))
    # symmetric smooth family

       coeff = rng.normal(0,0.4,size=deg+1)

       h = np.zeros(n)
       for j in range(deg+1): h += coeff[j]*t**j
       h = 0.5 + np.tanh(h)
       h = project_feasible(np.clip(h,0,1),n)

       lengths = rng.randint(1, max(2, n//m + 5), size=m)
       total=lengths.sum()
       # scale to n: create sequence by ratios
```

Function `binary_delta_search(h0, n, deadline, max_iter, seed)`:
Use top-level no helper? We can define inner? no. Implement all inside. Use top-level `apply_swap_delta`, `threshold_binary`, `compute_c5`, `project_feasible`.
Pseudo with no lambdas:
```
def binary_delta_search(h0, n, deadline, max_iter, seed):
    h = threshold_binary(h0, n)
    raw = np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, 'full').astype(np.float64)
    cur_max = int(np.rint(raw.max()))
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_raw = cur_max
    M = 2*n-1
    tmp = np.empty(M)
    h_rev = h[::-1]
    comp_rev = 1.0 - h_rev
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    ones = np.nonzero(h>0.5)[0]
    zeros = np.nonzero(h<0.5)[0]
    near = max(2, cur_max//10)
    cur_sec = int(np.sum(raw >= cur_max - near))
    no_improve = 0
    check = 0
    for it in range(max_iter):
        if time.time() > deadline: break
        if len(ones)==0 or len(zeros)==0: break
        if cur_max > best_raw:
            h = best_h.copy(); raw = np.correlate(h,1.0-h,'full'); cur_max=best_raw; ...
        if it % 50 == 0:
            crit = np.where(raw >= cur_max - near)[0]
        if len(crit)==0:
            crit = np.array([int(np.argmax(raw))])
        best_m = cur_max; best_sec = cur_sec; best_a=-1; best_b=-1
        attempts=0
        while attempts < 12:
            attempts +=1
            if rng.rand() < 0.08 or len(crit)==0:
                a = int(rng.choice(ones)); b = int(rng.choice(zeros))
            else:
                k = int(crit[rng.randint(len(crit))])
                L = k - (n-1)
                if L == 0:
                    continue
                if L > 0:
                    idxs = np.nonzero((h[L:n] > 0.5) & (h[0:n-L] < 0.5))[0]
                    if len(idxs)==0: continue
                    c = int(idxs[rng.randint(len(idxs))])
                    a = c + L; b = c
                else:
                    d = -L
                    idxs = np.nonzero((h[0:n-d] > 0.5) & (h[d:n] < 0.5))[0]
                    if len(idxs)==0: continue
                    c = int(idxs[rng.randint(len(idxs))])
                    a = c; b = c + d
            if h[a] <= 0.5 or h[b] >= 0.5:
                continue
            apply_swap_delta(raw, h, a, b, h_rev, comp_rev, tmp)
            m = int(np.rint(tmp.max()))
            if m < best_m or (m == best_m):
                sec = int(np.sum(tmp >= m - near))
                if m < best_m or sec < best_sec:
                    best_m=m; best_sec=sec; best_a=a; best_b=b
        if best_a >= 0:
            apply_swap_delta(raw, h, best_a, best_b, h_rev, comp_rev, tmp)
            raw = np.rint(tmp).copy()
            h[best_a]=0.0; h[best_b]=1.0
            h_rev = h[::-1]; comp_rev = 1.0 - h_rev
            cur_max = best_m
            cur_sec = int(np.sum(raw >= cur_max - near))
            ones = np.nonzero(h>0.5)[0]; zeros=np.nonzero(h<0.5)[0]
            no_improve = 0
            if cur_max < best_raw:
                best_raw = cur_max; best_h = h.copy()
            check += 1
            if check >= 64:
                check = 0
                raw2 = np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, 'full')
                m2 = int(np.rint(raw2.max()))
                if m2 != cur_max:
                    raw = np.rint(raw2); cur_max = m2; cur_sec=...
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 8000:
                h = best_h.copy()
                raw = np.correlate(h,1.0-h,'full')
                cur_max = best_raw
                h_rev=h[::-1]; comp_rev=1-h_rev
                ones=...; zeros=...
                no_improve=0
                # random perturbation: do up to 6 safe swaps
                for _ in range(6):
                   a=rng.choice(ones); b=rng.choice(zeros)
                   apply...
                   m=int(np.rint(tmp.max()))
                   if m <= cur_max:
                      accept raw=...; update h
    return best_h, float(best_raw * (2.0/n))
```

`threshold_binary`:
```
def threshold_binary(h, n):
    hb = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    order = np.argsort(h)[::-1]
    hb[order[:n//2]] = 1.0
    return hb
```
If n even. If h length different? use len h.

`resample`:
```
def resample(h, n_new):
    n_old = len(h)
    if n_old == n_new: return h.copy()
    xo = (2.0/n_old)*(np.arange(n_old)+0.5)
    xn = (2.0/n_new)*(np.arange(n_new)+0.5)
    return project_feasible(np.interp(xn, xo, h), n_new)
```

`_ST` keys: n, dx, alpha, target, bounds, lags.

`cons_fun`:
```
def cons_fun(x): return float(np.sum(x) - _ST['target'])
def cons_jac(x): return np.ones(_ST['n'])
```

`slsqp_polish`:
```
def slsqp_polish(h, n, alpha, maxiter, deadline):
    x0 = project_feasible(h, n)
    _ST['n']=n; _ST['dx']=2.0/n; _ST['alpha']=float(alpha); _ST['target']=n/2.0
    _ST['bounds']=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    _ST['lags']=np.arange(-(n-1), n, dtype=float)
    opts={'maxiter': int(max(10, maxiter)), 'ftol':1e-12, 'disp':0}
    try:
       res = minimize(soft_objgrad, x0, method='SLSQP', bounds=_ST['bounds'],
                      constraints=[{'type':'eq','fun':cons_fun,'jac':cons_jac}],
                      jac=True, options=opts)
       if res.x is not None and res.x.size == n:
          x1 = project_feasible(res.x, n)
          if compute_c5(x1) < 1e300: return x1
    except Exception:
       pass
    return x0
```

Let's think about `project_feasible` exact sum. It clips before sum. If h has values near 0/1, scaling may lose exact. We adjust last. Let's code carefully:
```
def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.clip(np.asarray(h, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0)
    target = float(n) / 2.0
    for _ in range(100):
        s = float(h.sum())
        diff = target - s
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13: break
        if diff > 0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = room.sum()
            if tot <= 1e-15: break
            h = np.clip(h + (diff / tot) * room, 0.0, 1.0)
        else:
            meas = h
            tot = meas.sum()
            if tot <= 1e-15: break
            h = np.clip(h + (diff / tot) * meas, 0.0, 1.0)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - float(h.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
        idx = int(np.argmin(np.abs(h - 0.5)))
        if diff > 0:
            add = min(diff, 1.0 - h[idx])
            h[idx] += add
            diff -= add
        else:
            add = max(diff, -h[idx])
            h[idx] += add
            diff -= add
        if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
            # distribute to up to 5 closest
            order = np.argsort(np.abs(h - 0.5))[:5]
            for i in order:
               if abs(diff) <= 1e-16: break
               if diff > 0:
                  add = min(diff, 1.0 - h[i]); h[i] += add; diff -= add
               else:
                  add = max(diff, -h[i]); h[i] += add; diff -= add
    return np.clip(h,0,1)
```
If one idx at bound, add=0; distribute. Good.

`binary_delta_search`: Need if `near` changes after cur_max update. In validation, after raw reset, near maybe update. We can recompute near at loop top.
Implementation details with `tmp` and `raw` copies:
```
def binary_delta_search(h0, n, deadline, max_iter, seed):
    h = threshold_binary(h0, n)
    raw = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, 'full').astype(float)
    cur_max = int(np.rint(raw.max()))
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_raw = cur_max
    M = 2*n - 1
    tmp = np.empty(M)
    h_rev = h[::-1]
    comp_rev = 1.0 - h_rev
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    ones = np.nonzero(h > 0.5)[0]
    zeros = np.nonzero(h < 0.5)[0]
    near = max(2, cur_max // 10)
    cur_sec = int(np.sum(raw >= cur_max - near))
    no_improve = 0
    check = 0
    for it in range(max_iter):
        if time.time() > deadline: break
        if len(ones) == 0 or len(zeros) == 0: break
        near = max(2, cur_max // 10)
        if cur_max > best_raw:
            h = best_h.copy()
            raw = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, 'full').astype(float)
            cur_max = best_raw
            h_rev = h[::-1]; comp_rev = 1.0 - h_rev
            ones = np.nonzero(h > 0.5)[0]; zeros = np.nonzero(h < 0.5)[0]
        if it % 50 == 0:
            crit = np.nonzero(raw >= cur_max - near)[0]
        if len(crit) == 0:
            crit = np.nonzero(raw == raw.max())[0]
        allow = 0
        if no_improve > 3000: allow = 1
        if no_improve > 8000: allow = 2
        best_m = cur_max
        best_sec = int(np.sum(raw >= cur_max - near))
        best_a = -1; best_b = -1
        attempts = 0
        while attempts < 10:
            attempts += 1
            if len(crit) == 0 or rng.rand() < 0.08:
                a = int(rng.choice(ones)); b = int(rng.choice(zeros))
            else:
                k = int(crit[int(rng.randint(len(crit)))])
                L = k - (n - 1)
                if L == 0:
                    continue
                if L > 0:
                    idxs = np.nonzero((h[L:n] > 0.5) & (h[0:n - L] < 0.5))[0]
                    if len(idxs) == 0:
                        continue
                    c = int(idxs[int(rng.randint(len(idxs)))])
                    a = c + L; b = c
                else:
                    d = -L
                    idxs = np.nonzero((h[0:n - d] > 0.5) & (h[d:n] < 0.5))[0]
                    if len(idxs) == 0:
                        continue
                    c = int(idxs[int(rng.randint(len(idxs)))])
                    a = c; b = c + d
            if h[a] <= 0.5 or h[b] >= 0.5:
                continue
            apply_swap_delta(raw, h, a, b, h_rev, comp_rev, tmp)
            m = int(np.rint(tmp.max()))
            if m > cur_max + allow:
                continue
            sec = int(np.sum(tmp >= m - near))
            if m < best_m or (m == best_m and sec < best_sec):
                best_m = m; best_sec = sec; best_a = a; best_b = b
            elif m < cur_max:
                best_m = m; best_sec = sec; best_a = a; best_b = b
        if best_a >= 0:
            apply_swap_delta(raw, h, best_a, best_b, h_rev, comp_rev, tmp)
            raw = np.rint(tmp).copy()
            h[best_a] = 0.0; h[best_b] = 1.0
            h_rev = h[::-1]; comp_rev = 1.0 - h_rev
            cur_max = best_m
            cur_sec = int(np.sum(raw >= cur_max - near))
            ones = np.nonzero(h > 0.5)[0]; zeros = np.nonzero(h < 0.5)[0]
            no_improve = 0
            if cur_max < best_raw:
                best_raw = cur_max; best_h = h.copy()
            check += 1
            if check >= 64:
                check = 0
                raw2 = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, 'full')
                m2 = int(np.rint(raw2.max()))
                if m2 != cur_max:
                    raw = np.rint(raw2).astype(float)
                    cur_max = m2
                    cur_sec = int(np.sum(raw >= cur_max - max(2, cur_max//10)))
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 15000:
                h = best_h.copy()
                raw = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, 'full').astype(float)
                cur_max = best_raw
                h_rev = h[::-1]; comp_rev = 1.0 - h_rev
                ones = np.nonzero(h > 0.5)[0]; zeros=np.nonzero(h < 0.5)[0]
                no_improve = 0
    return best_h, float(best_raw * (2.0 / n))
```
Good.

```
if allow > 0 and m <= cur_max + allow and no_improve > 3000 and rng.rand() < 0.2:
    best_m=m; best_sec=sec; best_a=a; best_b=b
```

```
def slsqp_polish(h,n,alpha,maxiter,deadline,max_seconds=60):
    rem = max(1.0, min(deadline, time.time()+max_seconds) - time.time())

```

Compact project:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.clip(np.asarray(h,float),0,1); tgt=n/2.0
    for _ in range(80):
        d=tgt-h.sum()
        if abs(d)<1e-12: break
        if d>0:
            r=1-h; t=r.sum(); break if t<1e-14 else h=np.clip(h + d/t*r,0,1)
        else:
            t=h.sum(); break if t<1e-14 else h=np.clip(h + d/t*h,0,1)
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    d=tgt-h.sum()
    if abs(d)>1e-13:
        i=int(np.argmin(np.abs(h-0.5)))
        if d>0: h[i]=min(1,h[i]+d); d-=h[i]-...? Hard in one line.
```
Maybe easier loops. But concise.

Full code draft:
```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

_ST = {}

def score_key(x):
    return x[0]

def compute_c5(h):
    n = len(h)
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, 'full')) * (2.0 / n))

def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.clip(np.asarray(h, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0)
    target = n / 2.0
    for _ in range(80):
        diff = target - float(h.sum())
        if abs(diff) < 1e-12:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = float(room.sum())
            if tot < 1e-14:
                break
            h = np.clip(h + (diff / tot) * room, 0.0, 1.0)
        else:
            meas = h
            tot = float(meas.sum())
            if tot < 1e-14:
                break
            h = np.clip(h + (diff / tot) * meas, 0.0, 1.0)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - float(h.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 1e-13:
        for i in np.argsort(np.abs(h - 0.5))[:8]:
            if abs(diff) < 1e-15:
                break
            if diff > 0:
                add = min(diff, 1.0 - h[i])
            else:
                add = max(diff, -h[i])
            h[i] += add
            diff -= add
    return np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
```
Line count ~30. Good.

`threshold_binary` 5.
`resample` 8.

`generate_starts`:
```
def generate_starts(n, seed, base):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    xs = 2.0 * (np.arange(n) + 0.5) / n
    starts = []
    if base is not None:
        b = project_feasible(base, n)
        add_start(starts, b, n)
        add_start(starts, 0.5 * (b + b[::-1]), n)
        add_start(starts, threshold_binary(b, n), n)
    add_start(starts, 1.0 - np.abs(xs - 1.0), n)
    add_start(starts, 4.0 * xs * (1.0 - xs), n)
    add_start(starts, np.sin(0.5 * np.pi * xs) ** 2, n)
    add_start(starts, np.cos(0.5 * np.pi * xs) ** 2, n)
    amps = (0.25, 0.45, 0.65)
    for a in amps:
        for m in (1, 2, 3):
            ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
            add_start(starts, 0.5 + a * np.cos(m * np.pi * xs + ph), n)
    for _ in range(4):
        c = rng.normal(0.0, 0.5, rng.randint(3, 6))
        t = 2.0 * xs - 1.0
        y = np.ones(n)
        for j in range(c.size):
            y += c[j] * t ** j
        add_start(starts, 0.5 + np.tanh(2.0 * (y - 1.0)), n)
    for _ in range(6):
        m = int(rng.randint(3, 12))
        cuts = np.sort(rng.choice(np.arange(1, n), size=m - 1, replace=False))
        cuts = np.insert(cuts, 0, 0)
        vb = (rng.rand(m) < 0.5).astype(float)
        hb = np.zeros(n)
        for b in range(m):
            s = int(cuts[b]); e = int(cuts[b + 1]) if b + 1 < m else n
            if vb[b] > 0.5:
                hb[s:e] = 1.0
        add_start(starts, threshold_binary(hb, n), n)
    for _ in range(4):
        m = int(rng.randint(3, 10))
        cuts = np.sort(rng.choice(np.arange(1, n), size=m - 1, replace=False))
        cuts = np.insert(cuts, 0, 0)
        vals = (rng.rand(m) * 1.0)
        hb = np.zeros(n)
        for b in range(m):
            s = int(cuts[b]); e = int(cuts[b + 1]) if b + 1 < m else n
            hb[s:e] = vals[b]
        add_start(starts, hb, n)
    for _ in range(4):
        w = 0.2 + 0.4 * rng.rand()
        g1 = np.exp(-((xs - 1.0) ** 2) / (2.0 * w * w))
        add_start(starts, g1, n)
        g2 = np.exp(-((xs - 0.5) ** 2) / (2.0 * w * w)) + np.exp(-((xs - 1.5) ** 2) / (2.0 * w * w))
        add_start(starts, g2, n)
    return starts[:24]
```
This ~45 lines. OK. We might not need both binary/varying blocks if line count, but fine.

Soft_objgrad ~40.

SLSQP:
```
def soft_objgrad(x):
    n = _ST['n']; dx = _ST['dx']; alpha = float(_ST['alpha'])
    h = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    if h.size != n:
        h = np.resize(h, n)
    raw = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, 'full')
    g = raw * dx
    gm = float(np.max(g))
    z = alpha * (g - gm)
    ev = np.exp(z - np.max(z))
    s = float(ev.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return gm, np.zeros(n)
    val = gm + np.log(s) / alpha
    grad = np.zeros(n)
    w = ev / s
    lags = _ST['lags']
    for i in np.nonzero(w > 1e-14)[0]:
        L = int(lags[i]); a = float(w[i]) * dx
        if L >= 0:
            if L == 0:
                grad[:] += a * (1.0 - 2.0 * h)
            else:
                grad[L:n] += a * (1.0 - h[:n - L])
                grad[:n - L] -= a * h[L:n]
        else:
            d = -L
            grad[:n - d] += a * (1.0 - h[d:n])
            grad[d:n] -= a * h[:n - d]
    return val, grad
```
If h outside [0,1], raw maybe. We might want clip? But SLSQP bounds. Could if `np.any(h < -1e-8) or ...` h=np.clip(h,0,1). Add 1 line maybe. If h outside and 1-h >1, objective weird. SLSQP with bounds should not. Add:
```
    if h.min() < 0.0 or h.max() > 1.0:
```

`slsqp_polish`:
```
def slsqp_polish(h, n, alpha, maxiter, deadline, max_seconds=45.0):
    x0 = project_feasible(h, n)
    _ST['n'] = n; _ST['dx'] = 2.0 / n; _ST['alpha'] = float(alpha); _ST['target'] = n / 2.0
    _ST['bounds'] = [(0.0, 1.0)] for? Need entire list: `[ (0.0, 1.0) for _ in range(n) ]` list comp allowed? no lambda. Use for loop to avoid? List comp okay.
    _ST['lags'] = np.arange(-(n - 1), n, dtype=int)
    rem = max(1.0, min(deadline, time.time() + max_seconds) - time.time())
    mi = int(min(maxiter, max(10, rem / 0.35)))
    try:
        res = minimize(soft_objgrad, x0, method='SLSQP', bounds=_ST['bounds'],
              constraints=[{'type': 'eq', 'fun': cons_fun, 'jac': cons_jac}],
              jac=True, options={'maxiter': mi, 'ftol': 1e-12, 'disp': 0})
        if res.x is not None and res.x.size == n:
            return project_feasible(res.x, n)
    except Exception:
        pass
    return x0
```

`binary_delta_search` compact. Need `crit` update if `raw >= cur_max - near` but near may int. Use `int` conversions.

Let's code binary carefully, no long lines too much.

```
def apply_swap_delta(raw, h, a, b, h_rev, comp_rev, out):
    n = len(h)
    np.copyto(out, raw)
    out[a:a + n] -= comp_rev
    out[n - 1 - a:2 * n - 1 - a] += h
    out[n - 1] -= 1.0
    out[b:b + n] += comp_rev
    out[n - 1 - b:2 * n - 1 - b] -= h
    out[n - 1] -= 1.0
    out[n - 1 + a - b] += 1.0
    out[n - 1 + b - a] += 1.0
    return out
```
Check slice `2*n-1-a` for a=0: 2n-1 = M, okay. For a=n-1: 2n-1-(n-1)=n, lower n-1-(n-1)=0; length n. Good.

Binary maybe line ~90. We'll tighten.

```
def binary_delta_search(h0, n, deadline, max_iter, seed):
    h = threshold_binary(h0, n)
    raw = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, 'full').astype(float)
    cur = int(np.rint(raw.max())); best_raw = cur; best_h = h.copy()
    M = 2 * n - 1; tmp = np.empty(M)
    h_rev = h[::-1]; comp = 1.0 - h_rev
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    ones = np.nonzero(h > 0.5)[0]; zeros = np.nonzero(h < 0.5)[0]
    noimp = 0; check = 0
    for it in range(max_iter):
        if time.time() > deadline or len(ones) == 0 or len(zeros) == 0: break
        if cur > best_raw:
            h = best_h.copy(); raw = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, 'full').astype(float)
            cur = best_raw; h_rev = h[::-1]; comp = 1.0 - h_rev
            ones = np.nonzero(h > 0.5)[0]; zeros = np.nonzero(h < 0.5)[0]
        near = max(2, cur // 10)
        if it % 50 == 0:
            crit = np.nonzero(raw >= cur - near)[0]
        if len(crit) == 0:
            crit = np.nonzero(raw == raw.max())[0]
        allow = 0
        if noimp > 3000: allow = 1
        if noimp > 8000: allow = 2
        bm = cur; bsec = int(np.sum(raw >= cur - near)); ba = -1; bb = -1
        for tr in range(10):
            if len(crit) == 0 or rng.rand() < 0.08:
                a = int(rng.choice(ones)); b = int(rng.choice(zeros))
            else:
                k = int(crit[int(rng.randint(len(crit)))]); L = k - (n - 1)
                if L == 0: continue
                if L > 0:
                    idx = np.nonzero((h[L:n] > 0.5) & (h[0:n - L] < 0.5))[0]
                    if not idx.size: continue
                    c = int(idx[int(rng.randint(idx.size))]); a = c + L; b = c
                else:
                    d = -L
                    idx = np.nonzero((h[0:n - d] > 0.5) & (h[d:n] < 0.5))[0]
                    if not idx.size: continue
                    c = int(idx[int(rng.randint(idx.size))]); a = c; b = c + d
            if h[a] <= 0.5 or h[b] >= 0.5: continue
            apply_swap_delta(raw, h, a, b, h_rev, comp, tmp)
            m = int(np.rint(tmp.max()))
            if m > cur + allow: continue
            sec = int(np.sum(tmp >= m - near))
            if m < bm or (m == bm and sec < bsec) or (allow and m <= cur + allow and rng.rand() < 0.25):
                bm = m; bsec = sec; ba = a; bb = b
        if ba >= 0:
            apply_swap_delta(raw, h, ba, bb, h_rev, comp, tmp)
            raw = np.rint(tmp).copy()
            h[ba] = 0.0; h[bb] = 1.0
            h_rev = h[::-1]; comp = 1.0 - h_rev
            cur = bm; noimp = 0
            ones = np.nonzero(h > 0.5)[0]; zeros = np.nonzero(h < 0.5)[0]
            if cur < best_raw:
                best_raw = cur; best_h = h.copy()
            check += 1
            if check >= 64:
                check = 0
                r2 = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, 'full')
                m2 = int(np.rint(r2.max()))
                if m2 != cur:
                    raw = np.rint(r2).astype(float); cur = m2
        else:
            noimp += 1
            if noimp > 15000:
                h = best_h.copy(); raw = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, 'full').astype(float)
                cur = best_raw; h_rev = h[::-1]; comp = 1.0 - h_rev
                ones = np.nonzero(h > 0.5)[0]; zeros = np.nonzero(h < 0.5)[0]
                noimp = 0
    return best_h, float(best_raw * (2.0 / n))
```
Good. Note in `allow` branch, if m=cur+2, bm maybe >cur and accepted; after accept cur increases. OK. But bsec initial `raw >= cur - near`; if m>cur, sec threshold lower, sec maybe high; random chance. Fine.

```
def generate_starts(n, seed, base):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    xs = 2.0 * (np.arange(n) + 0.5) / n
    st = []
    if base is not None:
        b = project_feasible(base, n)
        st.extend([b, project_feasible(0.5*(b+b[::-1]), n), threshold_binary(b, n)])
    for v in (1.0 - np.abs(xs-1.0), 4.0*xs*(1.0-xs), np.sin(0.5*np.pi*xs)**2, np.cos(0.5*np.pi*xs)**2):
        add_start(st, v, n)
    for amp in (0.25,0.45,0.65):
        for m in (1,2,3):
            ph = rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi); add_start(st, 0.5+amp*np.cos(m*np.pi*xs+ph), n)
    for _ in range(5):
        m=int(rng.randint(3,12)); cuts=np.sort(rng.choice(np.arange(1,n),m-1,replace=False)); cuts=np.insert(cuts,0,0)
        vals=rng.rand(m); h=np.zeros(n)
        for b in range(m):
            s=int(cuts[b]); e=int(cuts[b+1]) if b+1<m else n; h[s:e]=vals[b]
        add_start(st, threshold_binary(h,n), n); add_start(st, h,n)
    for _ in range(3):
        w=0.2+0.5*rng.rand(); g=np.exp(-((xs-1)**2)/(2*w*w)); add_start(st,g,n)
        g=np.exp(-((xs-0.5)**2)/(2*w*w))+np.exp(-((xs-1.5)**2)/(2*w*w)); add_start(st,g,n)
    return st[:20]
```

Potential issue in binary candidate for L>0: Our raw_L corresponds to p=i+L. We choose h[L:n]>0.5 and h[0:n-L]<0.5 -> a=c+L, b=c. Swapping a,b. Good. For L<0 d: raw_{-d} = ∑ h_i(1-h_{i+d}) with i=p? Since L=-d: p=i-d, q=i; condition h[p]=1,h[q]=0, q range d..n-1, p 0..n-d-1. We choose h[0:n-d]>0.5 & h[d:n]<0.5; a=c; b=c+d. Good.

Now, if `h` binary but `h[L:n]` float 1/0, comparisons ok.

Potential issue: In binary search, after allowed deterioration, `bm` may be >cur, `bsec` threshold lower. It may pick a worsening candidate with random. After accept, `cur=bm`, `noimp=0`. Fine. But `best_h` only updated if cur<best_raw; if cur increased, best remains. If no improvement from worsened state for 15000, reset. OK.

Could allowed deterioration lead to many pathological? reset.

Now, if `apply_swap_delta` produces raw with values not close to integer due float, `np.rint` values become float integers. `raw.max()` float. Good.

Now, I should consider if the binary delta formula `self -=1` each flip is correct for continuous? Only used binary. Yes.

Now final code line count. Let's approximate final after trimming. I will write concise but not too compressed. Need ensure char <12000. Around 9000 maybe.

Let's now produce final.
</think>

```python
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

_ST = {}

def score_key(x):
    return x[0]

def compute_c5(h):
    n = len(h)
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, 'full')) * (2.0 / n))

def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.clip(np.asarray(h, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0)
    target = float(n) / 2.0
    for _ in range(80):
        diff = target - float(h.sum())
        if abs(diff) < 1e-12:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = float(room.sum())
            if tot < 1e-14:
                break
            h = np.clip(h + (diff / tot) * room, 0.0, 1.0)
        else:
            tot = float(h.sum())
            if tot < 1e-14:
                break
            h = np.clip(h + (diff / tot) * h, 0.0, 1.0)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - float(h.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 1e-13:
        for i in np.argsort(np.abs(h - 0.5))[:8]:
            if abs(diff) < 1e-15:
                break
            if diff > 0.0:
                add = min(diff, 1.0 - h[i])
            else:
                add = max(diff, -h[i])
            h[i] += add
            diff -= add
    return np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)

def threshold_binary(h, n):
    hb = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    order = np.argsort(h)[::-1]
    hb[order[:n // 2]] = 1.0
    return hb

def resample(h, n_new):
    n_old = len(h)
    if n_old == n_new:
        return h.copy()
    xo = (2.0 / n_old) * (np.arange(n_old) + 0.5)
    xn = (2.0 / n_new) * (np.arange(n_new) + 0.5)
    return project_feasible(np.interp(xn, xo, h), n_new)

def add_start(st, h, n):
    if len(st) >= 24:
        return
    st.append(project_feasible(h, n))

def generate_starts(n, seed, base):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    xs = 2.0 * (np.arange(n) + 0.5) / n
    st = []
    if base is not None:
        b = project_feasible(base, n)
        st.append(b)
        st.append(project_feasible(0.5 * (b + b[::-1]), n))
        st.append(threshold_binary(b, n))
    for v in (
        1.0 - np.abs(xs - 1.0),
        4.0 * xs * (1.0 - xs),
        np.sin(0.5 * np.pi * xs) ** 2,
        np.cos(0.5 * np.pi * xs) ** 2,
        np.sin(np.pi * xs) ** 2,
    ):
        add_start(st, v, n)
    for amp in (0.25, 0.45, 0.65):
        for m in (1, 2, 3):
            ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
            add_start(st, 0.5 + amp * np.cos(m * np.pi * xs + ph), n)
    for _ in range(7):
        m = int(rng.randint(3, 12))
        cuts = np.sort(rng.choice(np.arange(1, n), m - 1, replace=False))
        cuts = np.insert(cuts, 0, 0)
        vals = rng.rand(m)
        h = np.zeros(n)
        for b in range(m):
            s = int(cuts[b])
            e = int(cuts[b + 1]) if b + 1 < m else n
            h[s:e] = vals[b]
        add_start(st, h, n)
        add_start(st, threshold_binary(h, n), n)
    for _ in range(3):
        w = 0.2 + 0.5 * rng.rand()
        g1 = np.exp(-((xs - 1.0) ** 2) / (2.0 * w * w))
        add_start(st, g1, n)
        g2 = np.exp(-((xs - 0.5) ** 2) / (2.0 * w * w)) + np.exp(-((xs - 1.5) ** 2) / (2.0 * w * w))
        add_start(st, g2, n)
    return st

def soft_objgrad(x):
    n = _ST['n']
    dx = _ST['dx']
    alpha = float(_ST['alpha'])
    h = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    if h.size != n:
        h = np.resize(h, n)
    if h.min() < 0.0 or h.max() > 1.0:
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    raw = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, 'full')
    g = raw * dx
    gm = float(np.max(g))
    z = alpha * (g - gm)
    ev = np.exp(z - np.max(z))
    s = float(ev.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return gm, np.zeros(n)
    val = gm + np.log(s) / alpha
    grad = np.zeros(n)
    w = ev / s
    lags = _ST['lags']
    for i in np.nonzero(w > 1e-14)[0]:
        L = int(lags[i])
        a = float(w[i]) * dx
        if L >= 0:
            if L == 0:
                grad[:] += a * (1.0 - 2.0 * h)
            else:
                grad[L:n] += a * (1.0 - h[:n - L])
                grad[:n - L] -= a * h[L:n]
        else:
            d = -L
            grad[:n - d] += a * (1.0 - h[d:n])
            grad[d:n] -= a * h[:n - d]
    return val, grad

def cons_fun(x):
    return float(np.sum(x) - _ST['target'])

def cons_jac(x):
    return np.ones(_ST['n'])

def slsqp_polish(h, n, alpha, maxiter, deadline, max_seconds=45.0):
    x0 = project_feasible(h, n)
    if deadline - time.time() < 3.0:
        return x0
    _ST['n'] = n
    _ST['dx'] = 2.0 / n
    _ST['alpha'] = float(alpha)
    _ST['target'] = float(n) / 2.0
    _ST['bounds'] = [(0.0, 1.0) for _ in range(n)]
    _ST['lags'] = np.arange(-(n - 1), n, dtype=int)
    rem = max(1.0, min(deadline, time.time() + max_seconds) - time.time())
    mi = int(min(maxiter, max(10, rem / 0.35)))
    try:
        res = minimize(soft_objgrad, x0, method='SLSQP', bounds=_ST['bounds'],
                       constraints=[{'type': 'eq', 'fun': cons_fun, 'jac': cons_jac}],
                       jac=True, options={'maxiter': mi, 'ftol': 1e-12, 'disp': 0})
        if res.x is not None and res.x.size == n:
            return project_feasible(res.x, n)
    except Exception:
        pass
    return x0

def apply_swap_delta(raw, h, a, b, h_rev, comp_rev, out):
    n = len(h)
    np.copyto(out, raw)
    out[a:a + n] -= comp_rev
    out[n - 1 - a:2 * n - 1 - a] += h
    out[n - 1] -= 1.0
    out[b:b + n] += comp_rev
    out[n - 1 - b:2 * n - 1 - b] -= h
    out[n - 1] -= 1.0
    out[n - 1 + a - b] += 1.0
    out[n - 1 + b - a] += 1.0
    return out

def binary_delta_search(h0, n, deadline, max_iter, seed):
    h = threshold_binary(h0, n)
    raw = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, 'full').astype(float)
    cur = int(np.rint(raw.max()))
    best_raw = cur
    best_h = h.copy()
    M = 2 * n - 1
    tmp = np.empty(M)
    h_rev = h[::-1]
    comp = 1.0 - h_rev
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    ones = np.nonzero(h > 0.5)[0]
    zeros = np.nonzero(h < 0.5)[0]
    noimp = 0
    check = 0
    for it in range(max_iter):
        if time.time() > deadline or len(ones) == 0 or len(zeros) == 0:
            break
        if cur > best_raw:
            h = best_h.copy()
            raw = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, 'full').astype(float)
            cur = best_raw
            h_rev = h[::-1]
            comp = 1.0 - h_rev
            ones = np.nonzero(h > 0.5)[0]
            zeros = np.nonzero(h < 0.5)[0]
        near = max(2, cur // 10)
        if it % 50 == 0:
            crit = np.nonzero(raw >= cur - near)[0]
        if len(crit) == 0:
            crit = np.nonzero(raw == raw.max())[0]
        allow = 0
        if noimp > 3000:
            allow = 1
        if noimp > 8000:
            allow = 2
        bm = cur
        bsec = int(np.sum(raw >= cur - near))
        ba = -1
        bb = -1
        for _ in range(10):
            if len(crit) == 0 or rng.rand() < 0.08:
                a = int(rng.choice(ones))
                b = int(rng.choice(zeros))
            else:
                k = int(crit[int(rng.randint(len(crit)))])
                L = k - (n - 1)
                if L == 0:
                    continue
                if L > 0:
                    idx = np.nonzero((h[L:n] > 0.5) & (h[0:n - L] < 0.5))[0]
                    if not idx.size:
                        continue
                    c = int(idx[int(rng.randint(idx.size))])
                    a = c + L
                    b = c
                else:
                    d = -L
                    idx = np.nonzero((h[0:n - d] > 0.5) & (h[d:n] < 0.5))[0]
                    if not idx.size:
                        continue
                    c = int(idx[int(rng.randint(idx.size))])
                    a = c
                    b = c + d
            if h[a] <= 0.5 or h[b] >= 0.5:
                continue
            apply_swap_delta(raw, h, a, b, h_rev, comp, tmp)
            m = int(np.rint(tmp.max()))
            if m > cur + allow:
                continue
            sec = int(np.sum(tmp >= m - near))
            if m < bm or (m == bm and sec < bsec) or (allow and m <= cur + allow and rng.rand() < 0.25):
                bm = m
                bsec = sec
                ba = a
                bb = b
        if ba >= 0:
            apply_swap_delta(raw, h, ba, bb, h_rev, comp, tmp)
            raw = np.rint(tmp).copy()
            h[ba] = 0.0
            h[bb] = 1.0
            h_rev = h[::-1]
            comp = 1.0 - h_rev
            cur = bm
            noimp = 0
            ones = np.nonzero(h > 0.5)[0]
            zeros = np.nonzero(h < 0.5)[0]
            if cur < best_raw:
                best_raw = cur
                best_h = h.copy()
            check += 1
            if check >= 64:
                check = 0
                r2 = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, 'full')
                m2 = int(np.rint(r2.max()))
                if m2 != cur:
                    raw = np.rint(r2).astype(float)
                    cur = m2
        else:
            noimp += 1
            if noimp > 15000:
                h = best_h.copy()
                raw = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, 'full').astype(float)
                cur = best_raw
                h_rev = h[::-1]
                comp = 1.0 - h_rev
                ones = np.nonzero(h > 0.5)[0]
                zeros = np.nonzero(h < 0.5)[0]
                noimp = 0
    return best_h, float(best_raw * (2.0 / n))

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    B = float(budget_s)
    deadline = t0 + max(1.0, B - 3.0)
    try:
        h0 = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n = len(h0)
    except Exception:
        n = 250
        h0 = np.full(n, 0.5)
    if n > 400:
        h0 = resample(h0, 250)
        n = 250
    if n < 42 or (n % 2 != 0):
        if n < 42:
            n = 250
            h0 = np.full(n, 0.5)
        else:
            n -= 1
            h0 = h0[:n]
    h0 = project_feasible(h0, n)
    best = h0.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5(best)
    if B < 90:
        hb, cb = binary_delta_search(h0, n, deadline, 5000, seed)
        if cb < best_c:
            best, best_c = hb.copy(), cb
        best = project_feasible(best, len(best))
        return best, compute_c5(best), len(best)

    cands = [(best_c, best.copy())]
    starts = generate_starts(n, seed, best)
    scored = []
    quick_dl = t0 + 0.20 * B
    for s in starts:
        if time.time() > quick_dl:
            break
        scored.append((compute_c5(s), s))
    scored.sort(key=score_key)
    for i in range(min(6, len(scored))):
        if time.time() > quick_dl:
            break
        h2 = slsqp_polish(scored[i][1], n, 50.0, 35, quick_dl, 25.0)
        c2 = compute_c5(h2)
        cands.append((c2, h2.copy()))
        hb = threshold_binary(h2, n)
        cands.append((compute_c5(hb), hb.copy()))
    cands.sort(key=score_key)
    if best_c <= cands[0][0]:
        cands.insert(0, (best_c, best.copy()))
    cands = cands[:12]

    mid_dl = t0 + 0.55 * B
    for al in (100.0, 500.0, 2000.0, 10000.0, 50000.0, 200000.0):
        if time.time() >= mid_dl:
            break
        for cv, u in cands[:3]:
            if time.time() >= mid_dl:
                break
            h2 = slsqp_polish(u, n, al, 60, mid_dl, 40.0)
            c2 = compute_c5(h2)
            cands.append((c2, h2.copy()))
            hb = threshold_binary(h2, n)
            cands.append((compute_c5(hb), hb.copy()))
            if c2 < best_c:
                best, best_c = h2.copy(), c2
            if len(cands) > 18:
                cands.sort(key=score_key)
                cands = cands[:12]
        cands.sort(key=score_key)
        if best_c <= cands[0][0]:
            cands.insert(0, (best_c, best.copy()))
        cands = cands[:12]

    bin_dl = t0 + 0.82 * B
    pool = [best]
    for cv, u in cands[:3]:
        if len(pool) < 4:
            pool.append(u.copy())
    rem = max(0.0, bin_dl - time.time())
    for idx, st in enumerate(pool):
        if time.time() >= bin_dl or rem <= 0:
            break
        per = rem / max(1, len(pool) - idx)
        sub_dl = min(bin_dl, time.time() + max(25.0, per))
        hb, cb = binary_delta_search(st, n, sub_dl, 300000, seed + 31 + 17 * idx)
        if cb < best_c:
            best, best_c = hb.copy(), cb
        cands.append((cb, hb.copy()))
        cands.sort(key=score_key)
        cands = cands[:12]
        rem = max(0.0, bin_dl - time.time())

    cur_n = len(best)
    if cur_n < 300 and time.time() < deadline - 220.0:
        n2 = 300
        h2 = resample(best, n2)
        h2 = slsqp_polish(h2, n2, 100.0, 40, deadline - 80.0, 40.0)
        c2 = compute_c5(h2)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best, best_c = h2.copy(), c2
        if time.time() < deadline - 80.0:
            hb, cb = binary_delta_search(h2, n2, min(deadline - 80.0, time.time() + 120.0), 300000, seed + 777)
            if cb < best_c:
                best, best_c = hb.copy(), cb
        if time.time() < deadline - 50.0:
            h2 = slsqp_polish(h2, n2, 50000.0, 50, deadline - 30.0, 40.0)
            c2 = compute_c5(h2)
            if c2 < best_c:
                best, best_c = h2.copy(), c2

    fin_dl = deadline - 2.0
    final_pool = [(best_c, best.copy())]
    final_len = len(best)
    for cv, u in cands:
        if len(u) == final_len and len(final_pool) < 5:
            final_pool.append((cv, u.copy()))
    final_pool.sort(key=score_key)
    final_pool = final_pool[:4]
    for al in (20000.0, 100000.0, 500000.0):
        if time.time() >= fin_dl:
            break
        for cv, u in final_pool[:2]:
            if time.time() >= fin_dl:
                break
            h2 = slsqp_polish(u, final_len, al, 70, fin_dl, 50.0)
            c2 = compute_c5(h2)
            if c2 < best_c:
                best, best_c = h2.copy(), c2
            hb = threshold_binary(h2, final_len)
            final_pool.append((compute_c5(hb), hb.copy()))
            final_pool.append((c2, h2.copy()))
            final_pool.sort(key=score_key)
            final_pool = final_pool[:5]
    if time.time() < fin_dl - 45.0:
        hb, cb = binary_delta_search(best, final_len, fin_dl - 5.0, 200000, seed + 999)
        if cb < best_c:
            best, best_c = hb.copy(), cb
        if time.time() < fin_dl - 5.0:
            h2 = slsqp_polish(hb, final_len, 200000.0, 40, fin_dl - 1.0, 30.0)
            c2 = compute_c5(h2)
            if c2 < best_c:
                best, best_c = h2.copy(), c2

    best = project_feasible(best, len(best))
    best_c = compute_c5(best)
    return best, best_c, len(best)
```
